- **paper2code**: Reproduce a research paper as a working code repository. ([parallel-web/parallel-agent-skills](https://github.com/parallel-web/parallel-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/parallel-web-search) · ★75 · 13k installs)
- **parallel-web-verification**: This skill should be used when verifying a local web application, especially a WebGL or Three.js application, from parallel agent workspaces. It defines how to attach to an existing workspace server, bound headless Playwright resources, isolate scenarios with browser contexts, collect deterministic diagnostics, and keep interactive browser use opt-in. ([bradleyberkman/agent-operating-policy](https://github.com/bradleyberkman/agent-operating-policy/tree/HEAD/skills/parallel-web-verification))
- **parallel-work**: Work on multiple stories at the same time. Creates isolated parallel streams (git worktrees) from the current branch, wires up local settings, and opens each in its own Claude Code session. Also shows stream status and cleans up finished streams. Pairs with /merge-up (publish a stream's work to the parent branch) and /merge-down (pull the parent's accumulated work into a stream). ([joris887/exosuit](https://github.com/joris887/exosuit/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/parallel-work) · ★7)
- **parallel-work**: Rules for working in a repository where other Claude sessions are editing at the same time — no git writes, no process kills, strict scope, and verification narrowed or deferred so a test run cannot corrupt another session's. Use when the user says another session or agent is running in this codebase, asks to work in parallel mode, or mentions "/parallel-work". ([gabrieldabbah/genesis](https://github.com/gabrieldabbah/genesis/tree/HEAD/skills/parallel-work) · ★4)
- **parallel-work**: Split independent coding-agent work safely when tasks do not overlap. ([Andersseen/agentyx](https://github.com/Andersseen/agentyx/tree/HEAD/packages/core/skills/parallel-work) · ★3)
- **parallel-work**: Orchestrate parallel implementation of multiple tasks using isolated git worktrees, background agents, and the OpenSpec workflow.
